A moved golf plan (a recall, a keep) is re-proven where it will stand by `GolfValidator.quickProblems` (ponds sealed, every witness replayed), as a Dropper is by its validator. The structural boot check of a golf layout an older planner made (or a Classic re-made from its seed) scans every hole's whole plot for unsealed ponds, decorative ones included, and keeps the course closed on a gap (a SEVERE line containing "a pond isn't sealed"; a new course is built). Shipping order: golf first (no Bedrock physics risk); the boat slot ships off until the owner has run the boat test strip (Gate 0) and a preview.

**Adventure Golf (`GolfPlanner` algo 3; `gen/golf`, Course Variety §3).** *Why it was samey:* each tier's list was shuffled once and hole k of a tier took the k-th template, so with about as many templates as holes every Golf of the Week dealt the same seven shapes in a new order. *The shapes:* `HoleTemplate` gains SAND_TRAP (E, M), HUMP (E), HILL (M), VOLCANO (H), TERRACES (M, H; a pond on H), DOGLEG_DOWN (M, H; a slime bank on M, a sunken bunker on H), TREE_GARDEN (E, M, H), POND_SIDE (E: a pond to look at beyond the wall; M: in play), ISLAND_POND (H), TWO_WAY (M, H: a short way past a pond, sand first on H, or a long dry way round a tree island) and CREEK (M), drawn by the reworked `Sketch` in half-block levels (-1 a sunken bunker at T - 0.5, 0 turf, up to 4 two blocks up); each declares its `features(tier)` (water, sand, height, trees, big drop, or a pond view) and its tee sign's `teeFeature(tier)` (`GenCopy.golfTee(hole, par, feature)`: lines 1-2 "HOLE n" / "Par p", lines 3-4 the feature's, "Hit the ball / to the flag!" for none). *The quota* (`Quota`, pure): deal k of `DEALS` (64; a random deal meets every target only about one time in ten, so 16 left one course in six short) shuffles every tier's list with its own fork, `order:<tier>:<k>`, counts the holes' first-attempt templates against the targets for the mix's size (7+ holes: water 2, sand 2, height 3, trees 1, big drop 1; 4-6: 1, 1, 2, 1, 0; 3: 0, 1, 1, 1 with a pond view counting as the tree hole, 0), each capped at what the mix's tiers can hold, and takes the first deal meeting all, else the one meeting most (lowest k on a tie); only the seed and mix go in, so rederive finds the same deal, and the plan summary ends with the quota line the course really got. `GolfPlanner.dry(slot)` keeps Tiny Golf from ever dealing a template with water in play, whatever its mix (Easy has none anyway). *The block model:* `PlanBlocks` and `LiveBlocks` read water, sand and leaves, and smooth sandstone is SLOW (soul sand's friction) only on a course that plays Adventure Golf's rules (`LiveBlocks.sandPlays`: a generated golf course whose tag's algo is `FIRST_SAND_ALGO` 3 or later, a Classics recall of one included, or a kept course with `GolfCourse.adventure`, written by `CourseCodec` as `adventure: true` when `KeepService` keeps a layout of algo 3 on); every hand-built course and older layout reads it as stone, so a course built by hand never plays differently (COURSE-VARIETY-DECISIONS 2). *The rules* (`GolfShot.Rules`, which `LiveBlocks` implements and the planner's blocks too): besides sand, a ball that comes to rest with its centre over water has fallen in (`GolfShot.settled` turns that STOPPED into WATER: the physics holds a ball up by the edge of its footprint, so without it a ball could stop hanging over a pond's edge, or float on a pond held by a wall's lower block); and the wobble (the owner's call, the CV final gate): a soft putt can wedge the ball's edge against a half-block step, where the physics lifts it a sliver and drops it back every sub-step with no sideways speed, so it hung just above the ground "rolling" until the 600-tick roll cap; on Adventure Golf a ball whose sideways speed stays under `STOP` and whose height stays within a sliver (`WOBBLE_BAND`) for `WOBBLE_TICKS` (20) ticks running settles (`BallPhysics.settle`) and stops there, in the cup if it rests on it, in the water if it sank into it. `GolfShot.Rolling` is the one tick loop that the proofs (`GolfShot.play`, so the expert search, `SafeExpert`, `KidPolicy` and `GolfValidatorV3`'s replays) and the live round (`LiveRound.roll`) share, so a proof and a player always see the same putt, and on any other course its window is never looked at. *The proof* (`GolfValidatorV3`, from the blocks alone): cup, tee and flag as before; every pond sealed (`Pools`), one deep at T - 1, every pond column in a 3 x 3 square of water (a full-power ball skims about 2.7 blocks), none beside the tee, within 2 of the cup ring, beside a slab or at the bounds' edge, every pond cell wading to a turf-level edge a child steps out onto, decorative water 2 columns clear of every hole; no lane at the bounds' edge or beside nothing but a pond; ring walls checked locally (more than half a block above the highest lane beside them, at most two above the lowest, and the rail rule for a ball riding a wall's lower block over lower lane or a pond) instead of one height all round; the flight rule (a low wall or obstacle stands further from a lip than a full-power ball flies before dropping enough for it to stop it: `LaneMap.flightReach`, with `LaneMap` v3 reading hazards and lanes up to T + 2); obstacles (trunks, tree islands) a block above the lane beside them; hollows only at the cup and in sunken bunkers; headroom 3 over every lane and no canopy within 2 of the tee, the cup ring or the flag; no stranding; nobody trapped; the witness replays in exactly E putts, par E + 1 (Easy 2-3, Medium and Hard 3-4); on a hole with water in play every putt of the witness is dry 3 degrees either side (`SafeExpert`, so par is the safe line); the sloppy player's tree always finishes within par + 1 and never gets wet (`KidPolicy`); and every spot a ball rests in either is lane. *Scenery* (`GolfScenery`): 2-5 trees on moss planters per plot, drawn from `scenery:<hole>` only after the hole is accepted, every block 2 columns outside the hole's bounds (outside the physics grid), so it can never change a proof or which attempt won. The planner otherwise works as before: attempts 0-5 on the hole's template, 6-11 the next of its tier, 12 `SAFE_STRAIGHT`, all inside `ATTEMPT_BUDGET` and the course's counted work.

**The Mountain Run (`BoatPlanner` algo 3; `gen/boat`, Course Variety §2).** *Why a sprint:* a boat can't climb (F1), so a closed loop can never lose height; everything the races use already handles a one-lap course (`Laps` natural 1, the grid straight back from the start, the stand at the half's middle). *The shape* (`TrackPath`, pure, `fork("track:" + t)`): a rounded-square spiral from the rim of the 128 x 16 x 128 half inward round the stand (the stand's rail at the half's top, so the start's ice is H0 + 8), four straights a turn so every drop lands on a straight, rings one pitch apart (20, 18, 16), 8 orientations and a seeded outer offset, inner corners near the tier's smallest radius so the inner straights hold a drop and its landing; exact on whole blocks (a column's nearest centreline point is found on the analytic straights and quarter arcs). *The heights* (`TrackProfile`): drops only on straights, each after a flat run-up and before a straight landing strip (22/26 blocks on packed ice, 33/41 on blue, for a 1- or 2-block drop), at least Z(d) + 12 apart and never inside another's flight zone, one leg each, so a checkpoint stands between every two; easy 4-5 drops of 1 (at most 5 down), medium 5-6 with at most two 2s, hard 4-6 with at most three (at most 7 down); the finish on the inner ring 12-30 from the stand's platform, with 14 blocks of run-out and a 6-block sand paddock. The Final Drop goes in front of the stand (43-70 before the finish) wherever a leg holds it, which on medium is about a third of seeds; easy's and hard's inner straights are shorter than a run-up and a landing strip, so their last drop is the last that fits, with its own HOP! or BIG DROP! sign and a checkpoint after it, and only a Final Drop with the finish next says "FINAL DROP! / Then the gold / finish line!" (COURSE-VARIETY-DECISIONS, 30 Sep; shorter landing strips from Gate 0 could change that in algo 4). *The pieces* (`TrackPieces`, a seeded deck per tier, each kept only while the checkpoints still fit round it): sand run-offs on the outside of every bend tighter than R 24 and kerbs on its inside (medium, hard); the sand pit (ice ways P wide past both sides); pick-a-path splits round a moss island with a tree (a sand patch on one branch on medium and hard); the Ice Cave (12-20 blocks under a light blue glass roof exactly 5 over the ice, sea lanterns in the walls); the forest slalom (medium, hard: 3 or 4 trunks, or one fewer where the checkpoints need room); boost strips (medium: 3 columns of blue ice, never within 30 before a drop); none in the first 40 blocks, on a run-up, landing or the finish, and sand pits, caves and boosts out of every flight zone, so they are commoner on hard. `TrackRaster` lays it on whole blocks with its walls, checkpoints (about every 32 along the track where one can go), markers (lime pit wall and grid rows, yellow caps and a light at every lip, light blue at checkpoints, gold at the finish, magenta arrows every 24) and signs (3-10 blocks before each drop, piece but a boost, and sandy bend); `BoatScenery` adds moss terraces between rings, the stepped summit cone under the stand and up to 28 trees, all 2 columns clear of the track and below the stand's floor. *The proof* (`DownhillValidator` V1-V13, from the blocks alone, on facts F1-F5 measured live in Gate 0, never on driving): the palette (no slime or water; easy packed ice only); one floor per column; downhill only (side by side, cells step down at most the tier's drop, every block reachable from the start without climbing, and every deck a boat can reach, `DeckGraph`, reaches the finish); containment (walls solid to 2 above the highest surface beside them, 2 above the lip round every flight zone, risers under 2-block drops, no open edge); widths (a P-wide path and an all-ice line from start to finish, so sand is never forced; every gap at least P); checkpoints (at least 3 in radius on flat open ice, cuts in order, at most one drop and 60 across the ground between two targets, at most 60 along with no drop, and a reset at each facing the next target within 60 degrees of the lane: `Course.resetYaw`, the one `TimeTrials.backTo` uses); headroom (4 over every drive cell, a cave roof exactly 5); falls (the tier's drop counts and totals); the stand (`LoopValidatorV2.standProblems` and nothing but the stand at its heights); the grid (the live `RaceGrid.plan` run on the plan's own blocks, `PlanSurface`, seats 12 in rows of two); the finish (a cut on the lowest deck, Z(d) + 3 from the Final Drop, 12-30 from the stand); times (`minSeconds` the course's legs' bound exactly); sizes (30,000 blocks, 64 keep-clear boxes). The flight zone comes from `BoatEnvelope`, integer arithmetic only: no boat goes faster than 3.75 blocks a tick (F3, `TrialKind.BOAT`'s 75 a second) and one in the air falls at least 0.04 a tick² (F4), so off d blocks it flies at most L(d) = 3.75 x ceil(1.3 t(d)) (the 1.3 a margin for Bedrock's client physics): L(1) 38, L(2) 49, zones Z(1) 40, Z(2) 51; a boat breaks off 3 or more, so 2 is the most. *Tries:* 20, each from its own streams (1-10 the whole deck, 11-15 no forest and one split and sand pit, 16-20 the drops, the bends' sand and the scenery), then `SAFE_SPIRAL` (the tier's smallest bends and fewest drops at fixed places, no pieces, proven for all 24 shapes in both halves), within `WORK_BUDGET` 2.5M. *No simulator yet* (COURSE-VARIETY-DECISIONS 1, the schedule valve): this is algo 3 without V14; the reference time is the centreline at 30 blocks a second and the landing strips are the spec's constants, and a `BoatSim` fun gate comes as algo 4 after Gate 0. A live algo-2 loop keeps its stored plan, judged by `LoopValidatorV2` and healed by its structure, until its set ends.

**Course Variety in the races and the admin tools (§5, §7, the CV final gate).** The race engine (`RaceGrid`, `RaceStand`, `Laps`, `RaceTrack`, `PartyRaces`, `FairPlay`, the Weekly Cup) needed no change; the Mountain Run gets copy only. `trial/BoatHype` (pure) says what a Mountain Run says about itself: `mountain(c)` is a Fresh boat tag of algo 3 on (a kept course's tag is gone, a flat loop is algo 2, so neither changes); `drops(c)` counts the legs (start to the first checkpoint, each checkpoint to the next, the last to the finish) whose next mark is at least half a block lower, which is the drop count exactly because the planner puts one checkpoint between every two lips and every mark sits on the ice; `finalDrop(c)` is the checkpoint before the last drop only when the finish is the next target after it, which is exactly where the planner put the FINAL DROP! sign. From it: the tile NAME's "5 drops · " before the stars (`TimeTrials.tileFact`, `DailyTiles.trialCount`, like the Dropper's levels), the big "Final drop!" title at that checkpoint (`TimeTrials.checkpointBig`), and Race Night's hype tail on the heads-up and join-open lines (`BoatHype.line`, "This week: 5 drops down the mountain!" in the set's cadence words, behind `games.race_night.hype`, shipped true, runtime copy only: R6). `EventCopy.downhill(track)` (a Mountain Run that isn't a loop) words a night "3 downhill races" (the Race Night screen, before the night too through `RaceNight.Upcoming`, and the status line), "a downhill sprint" in `event status`, no laps in the grid title (`gridLaps`), and "Warm-up runs" for the shared warm-up on the hub sign, screen, tile, view, watchers' bar and the racer's chat line (`EventCopy.warmup`); every other track reads as before (the old overloads stay). A solo warm-up's finish still rounds again from the start (`Warmups.lap`), so on a sprint each warm-up lap is a run from the top. *The admin tools* (CV final gate): a Fresh slot that is switched off (Ice Boat ships off) can be previewed, previewed next, test-run and walked (`GenService.readyToTry`: the area checks a build passes, at the command and again at the job's start, an unclaimed area scanned and claimed first, the spare half only, never a flip; `previewRun` refuses an off slot only for a standing problem, such as foreign blocks), and every reply says it stays off (`offNote`); `promote` and `choose` are refused while it is off (`offUse`: switching on always builds the set's own course, so a pick kept while off would not be what opens), the Admin tools hide them, and `on` over a standing or queued preview says the set's course is built there next (`onOverPreview`, `dropPreview`). `pin live` (and `today`) is refused when the live layout's algo isn't today's planner's (a pin is a seed for today's planner, so on an older layout it would build a different course "pinned"), naming `keep <course> current <new-id> confirm`; a typed seed matching an archived edition of another algo is pinned with a warning that names the edition and how to have it as it was (`olderEdition`, `asItWas`: it stays until its set ends and `keep ... current` while it is live; `recall <code>` when its Classics slot is on; else `keep <code>`). The archive grows with the boat: an algo-3 Mountain Run's stored plan is about 40-50 KB gzipped (some 20,000 ops); Mountain Run v2 (0.37 on, up to about 380,000 ops, stored with PlanCodec v2) is about 50-70 KB (0.37.0's plans measured 49-61 KB, 0.37.1's golden plans 54-65 KB), so a year of weekly sets for every course is a few MB with Ice Boat on (Ice Boat's own about 3 MB).
<!-- ---- end Course Variety ---- -->

**Leaderboards (EXTRAS E3).** On the website, `ArcadeFeed` reads every board-bearing entry's best rows itself (`ArcadeFeed.Boards`, after all games have written): a cabinet's from its `cabinet(...)` board, a course's from the board the game names with `FeedWriter.board(...)`, and a past Fresh course's (best 3) from Fresh Courses' own `freshHistory`; ranks are shared on ties (`FreshFeed.ranks`), and a name is looked up only while `arcade_show_names` is on. In game, a display bound to `@board:<id>[:<board>]` (`display/BoardDisplay`, pure, like `@news`) resolves the id to a cabinet's published board, a course's all-time board or a Fresh slot's live tag's board (so it follows each set), and is drawn on the display timer and again within a second of `Scores.onSubmit` naming its board: a one-second task redraws only the boards a score touched, never per tick.

**Where the games report a finish (EXTRAS E4).** The games tell `GamesService.tellProgress` (guarded) at each finish: `CabinetGame.finishClassic` (with whether the gold milestone was reached) and `finishDaily` (practice included), a counted time-trial run, a recorded golf round, and on a Fresh course the stars added to the week, the week's top goal crossed, and a whole live set finished (this run's stars being the last missing). A quit or friend game never reaches those calls, and no game of chance names `GameProgress`.

**Quests and achievements from the games (`games/GameProgress`, `arcade/GamesProgress`).** The skill games tell one listener at each finish, through `GamesService.tellProgress` (guarded: a listener that throws never breaks a game); games of chance never call it, and a cabinet finish is taken only from a catalog `CABINET`. `GamesProgress` turns a finish into one step of `FINISH_CABINET` (practice included), `FINISH_COURSE` (a counted run or a finished golf round) or `EARN_STARS` (one a star) and a few `player_counters` (`cabinet_finishes`, `cabinet_golds`, `cabinets` via a per-cabinet marker, `course_finishes`, `course_records`, `holes_in_one`, `golf_under_par`, `fresh_sets`, `star_chart_tops`). They count where the games pay tokens (`SkillRewards.canEarnHere`: an economy, Games or play world, never creative or spectator), not only in economy worlds: courses are played in the Games world, which is deliberately not one. Tokens still only move where the sandbox allows, so a quest reached in the Games world is banked and `QuestService.settle` pays it back home (every 30-second poll and on a world change), even once its day or week is over: it reads the player's unpaid rows of the game quests in any period (`QuestDao.unclaimed`, decided by the pure `QuestService.due`), and `markClaimed` flips each once. The "Games" achievements are all COUNTERs, which the join, five-minute and world-change sweeps unlock back home (the world-change sweep, like the others, only while `arcade.enabled`); an EVENT fired in the Games world could never pay. A game quest is dealt only while a game that can push it is open (`QuestService.draw` takes a filter), so nobody draws a quest they can't do. Config revision 17 appends the five pool rows and nine achievements to a list still exactly as shipped, and WARNs once with the lines to paste for one the owner changed.

**The setup check (`/hcm games check`, `command/GamesCheck`).** Read-only by construction: `GamesCheck` only decides and words, from a `Facts` interface of questions (no method is an action), and `GamesCheckLive` answers them from the config, the worlds, the Fresh Courses engine's read-only `GenService.report()`, the course rows and the website feed. One line per check, OK, WARN or FAIL with the fix, then "All good." or "N things to fix." (every WARN and FAIL). A section whose facts throw becomes one FAIL line and the rest still run, so it works with the games on, off or failed to start. Multiverse stays optional and is only read (plugin.yml loads this plugin *before* Multiverse-Core, so the built-in void generator is ready when Multiverse loads the Games world at every start; Multiverse is therefore not a declared soft dependency): a world's game mode comes from Multiverse-Core's API by reflection (5, then 4), and Multiverse-Inventories' groups and game-mode setting from its own `groups.yml` / `config.yml`; whatever can't be read says what to check by hand. The regions are checked with the same pure `Regions` rules the engine vets with (world height and border, other areas, hand-built courses; each kept plot too), then "What players can see" (the paragraph on where the places stand), the courses with their own `problems()` validators, and `/api/arcade` is built in memory the way the dashboard builds it, with each game's `feed` called directly rather than through the guard, so a feed that throws is reported instead of switching its game off.

**The new-courses line (`games/gen/NewCoursesNudge`).** "New courses this week! Easy, Parkour, Hard, Sky Rings and Golf - /hcm play", once per player per set (`games.fresh.announce`, shipped true). A set is the newest edition among the vouched-for, current layouts, without its reroll, so an admin's reroll isn't a new one. It waits until every switched-on course is up in it, or 15 minutes after its first (a failed build is retried later), then tells everyone who may play, is in a world the games are played in and isn't in a world session; someone on a screen hears it when they close it or after a minute, and someone who joins a few seconds after joining. The set told is kept in `game_prefs` (`news.fresh`), so a restart doesn't repeat it; `/hcm play news off` writes `news: off`. A pref that can't be read counts as told (never twice). It runs on Fresh Courses' one-second timer, reads the engine only through `GenService.report()`, and its pure parts (which set, the words, who is due) are tested with a fake host. No migration: `game_prefs` fits.

<!-- ---- dropper (WP-D) ---- -->
**The Dropper (EVENTS-DROPPER-SPEC §B.1).** A dropper is an ordinary `PlannedTrial` of kind `DROPPER` on two more slots (`fresh_dropper_easy`, `fresh_dropper`, a mix like golf's, shipped on inside `games.fresh`, which ships off) and a Classic Dropper, so rows, flip, archive, recall and keep are unchanged; its levels live in the course's own marks (start = ledge 1, checkpoints = pool, ledge, pool..., finish = the last pool; `DropMarks`, with `trial/DropperLayout` as Time Trials' face on it). The pure package `games/gen/dropper` plans and proves it (vanilla air physics, a witness and 171 pilots a level; the Dropper's level 5 is the Long Drop, the letter X: a 128-block fall through 8 plates in a 144-tall half, whose witness holds a key to the water so the hole line keeps sliding, proven at Hard's strength; `DropRules.mixProblem(mix, plots, sizeY)` refuses an X on a short half at the command, config revision 25 moves the shipped mix to EEMHX, and `TrialFinish`'s clean-run rule holds a Hard dropper's third star back from a bonked run); the engine admits still water only in a dropper's palette (`PlanCheck.paletteProblems`), proves a new plan against the mix it was asked for (`PlanCheck.generator`), re-proves a moved dropper on the planner thread (`movedProblems`, for a recall and a keep too) and builds each pass that may hold water (a dropper's slot or plot, or water seen while reading) in three global stages (`BuildJob`: unwanted water drained while the chunks are read, then every solid and sign, then the planned water bottom-up); every other pass, the other generators' and the arena's, writes chunk by chunk as before. `GenRegionGuard` cancels a flow whose source or target is in an area (`FLOW_OUT`, `FLOW_INTO`), kept Dropper plots included; a Dropper area whose `origin` moved without a `clear` stays guarded (`gen.<slot>.wet`, "drain first") until it is cleared; and Time Trials guards every Dropper course's pools itself (`DropperPools`, via `DropperHooks.flow`), so no pool spills while Fresh Courses is off. Fresh slots and the keep area stay 32 blocks from the Falling Floors box and the Clubhouse's to be built at all (`Regions.extrasProblem`, `keepExtrasProblem`, from `DailyCourses.extraBoxes`; the shipped spots are 576 apart, out of sight), on or off, in the vet, the keep and `/hcm games check`. In Time Trials the dropper diff is hook calls into `DropperHooks` (the server side: kits, fall-damage bonk, the player as a port) and the pure `DropperRun`/`DropperRules` (the practice drop on TrialRun's warm-up flags, the splash as the first move into a pool's splash box with `Progress.reachNext`, the box pulled in by half a body so a rim landing is a bonk (`DropMarks.splashBox`), the hop as the run's own teleport five ticks later, the bonk, which counts only when its teleport back works, and the no-push team and non-collision given back on every end); the Start tile's NAME says "Start - practice drop optional" while the practice drop is offered; `FairPlay` skips the legs that end at a ledge (the game's own hops). A clean counted drop is `GameProgress.dropperClean` (the `dropper_clean` counter, config revision 18).
<!-- ---- end dropper ---- -->

<!-- ---- events batch (EVENTS-DROPPER-SPEC, EVENTS-RECONCILED, EVENTS-OWNER-DECISIONS D1-D4) ---- -->
**The events batch.** Things to do together and a weekly goal, on the same house rules. What ships on: warm-ups (`games.trials.warmup_seconds: 180`), party races and golf together, the Weekly Cup (`games.cup.enabled: true`) and the two droppers inside `games.fresh` (which ships off). What ships off: Race Night (`games.race_night.enabled: false`) and Falling Floors (`games.falling_floors.enabled: false`). `GameCatalog.SPECS` gains `cup`, `race_night` and `falling_floors` (all `GameKind.TRIAL`); Race Night and Falling Floors sit on a sixth tab of the Games screen, **Together** (slot 7), shown only while one of them has a tile. Schema v36 adds `game_events`, `game_event_entries`, `game_event_races`, `cup_entries` and `cup_settlements`; config revision 18 appends the four event achievements (`game_dropper_clean`, `game_race_first`, `game_race_win`, `game_floors_minute`) to an unedited list, each only when missing, and WARNs with the lines to paste on an edited one (revision 17 is untouched). New ledger sources: `GAMES_DROPPER`, `GAMES_RACE_NIGHT`, `GAMES_FLOORS`, `GAMES_CUP_ENTRY`, `GAMES_CUP_PRIZE`, `GAMES_CUP_REFUND`; the Cup's are not chance sources, so Take a break never counts them. `Database.transaction` rolls back on any `Throwable` (an `Error` inside one used to reach `setAutoCommit(true)`, which commits). `EventsShippedDefaultsTest` pins what ships on and off (the Clubhouse's block and `games.trials.rider_runs_count` included), and `EventsCopyTest` scans every string literal of the batch (the Clubhouse's package and its bridges included) for the banned words, emoji and characters above U+FFFF.

**Warm-ups (D3; `trial/Warmup`, `Warmups`, `WarmupChoiceMenu`).** While `warmup_seconds` is above 0, starting a course first asks "Warm up (3:00)" or "Go straight to the timed run"; the choice is kept for that course only and forgotten on a quit, so it never starts a warm-up elsewhere. A warm-up is the run's one warm-up (`TrialRun.warmup`, `warmupUsed`): free laps from the start, checkpoints and Back to checkpoint working, and every finish routed by `RaceRun.route` to `WARMUP_LAP` before anything is judged, so no warm-up lap reaches `settleCounted` (no board, reward, Cup time or quest step). Its end (the clock, or the Start timed run kit item) is exactly a new run: back to the start and the normal 3-2-1. A race's shared warm-up is the same laps with a Ready item, ended by the coordinator's `regrid`. **Every warm-up ends at once in the restart hold**: a solo one goes to its 3-2-1, a party's or Race Night's straight to the grid. The Dropper never gets lap warm-ups: its warm-up is the practice drop (§ The Dropper), and `warmup_seconds: 0` turns both off.

**Race mode and party races (D4; `trial/RaceMode`, `RaceRun`, `RaceGrid`, `RaceStand`, `RaceLink`, `PartyRaces`, `PartyRace`, `Parties`).** Race mode is the one engine party races and Race Night share, as Time Trials runs with a `RaceRun` attached. A coordinator (`RaceLink`: a `PartyRace`, or Race Night's `NightRunner`) seats its racers through the normal world session (`TimeTrials.race`), and race mode holds them on one grid (`RaceGrid.forCourse`: rows of two behind the start line, else single file; runners and flyers on the start) until one go tick, so everyone starts on one clock; a racer who drifts off their spot before Go is put back and starts on the shared clock. Every tick `sweepArrivals` drops a racer whose trip to the track fell through, so nobody waits for them. A finish is judged on the course as it is (its layout, the still-standing rule for Fresh Courses) and goes to the link; finishers wait on the course's viewing stand (`RaceStand`, Fresh Ice Boat's built-in platform, used only while it really stands) or go home at the line. `reserve` holds a course for Race Night (new solo runs and party races refused) and calls off a party race already on it; holds are memory only, and Race Night re-reserves on every step. **Party races** (`/hcm play race <course>` or Race with friends, slot 20 on the course screen) use the shared parties (`Parties`: one party per player across party races and golf together, `party_max` 8): the host starts, the host picks the shared warm-up, and the race ends when everyone is in, 2 minutes after the first finish or 10 minutes after Go. Each racer's finish is also their **normal counted run**, once, through `settleCounted` (boards, rewards, quests, the Weekly Cup); a party pays nothing of its own. Never on a Dropper (`PartyRaces.offered`/`courseProblem`). Parkour and elytra racers are on the no-push team from seating until the run ends by any path; boats bump.

**Golf together (D4; `golf/GolfGroups`, `GolfGroup`, `GolfTogether`, `gui/games/golf/GolfGroupCardMenu`).** A golf party of up to 4 plays each hole at once, each with their own ball, and moves to the next tee together when every ball is in or picked up. `GolfGroups` sits behind a `Port` (tested without a server): once a second it lets go of a member whose trip never arrived (the others are told), and it records each player's round at their own last hole (`GolfRounds.finishLines`, on that day and set), once, so leaving or a disconnect after it keeps the round; the group's end only ranks and sends home. The **hole clock** (`GolfGroup.HOLE_CLOCK_SECONDS`, 120; on a Golf v4 layout, kept ones included, `GolfGroup.holeClock(par)` = max(120, 30 × (par + 1)) per hole, from `GolfGroup.clocks(course)`) starts when the first ball of a hole is in, while any ball is out; at 0 every ball still out is picked up at par + `max_over_par`. Each round is a normal round (boards, rewards); nothing extra for playing together.

**Race Night (§A, WP-R2; `games/event`).** Free boat races for everyone at once: `race_night.races` (3) races on one track, points in each, small server prizes. `EventSchedule` reads `"<days> <HH:mm>"` entries and keeps only nights whose whole worst-case window fits before a restart hold (and 15 minutes from a Fresh rebuild on a Fresh track); `EventMachine` is the pure state machine (SCHEDULED, OPEN, WARMUP, GRID, RACING, BREAK, SETTLING, DONE, CALLED_OFF) and `NightRunner` drives it through `NightPorts` (`LivePorts` on the server, straight to race mode). `Tracks` picks the track (`course: auto` takes turns among raceable boat courses by the night's id; Fresh Ice Boat is the default). Points by place (`NightRules`, `NightStandings`: countback on 1st then 2nd places, still-level racers share); the next grid puts the fewest points in front. **Prizes** (`RacePrizes`, EVENTS-RECONCILED 1, retuned by the 0.37 token balance for three races of 2-3 minutes): 1st 20, 2nd 12, 3rd 8, and 5 for every other finisher; 2nd needs 3 racers at race 1 and 3rd needs 4; a podium place needs a finished race and someone ranked strictly below; at most 30 tokens a player a night (`NightRules.MAX_PRIZE_PER_NIGHT`, 5 until 0.37; each prize 0-30, the finisher prize 0-10) and `prize_events_per_week` (3) prize nights a week server-wide. Prizes are `EVENT_PRIZE` rewards outside the skill caps, paid once per player per night (the ref is the night's id); an owed prize is paid by `PayLoop` a moment after the next join or on the framework's one-minute sweep (`Game.settleOwed`, which runs for every game that hasn't failed, open or switched off, so switching Race Night off after a prize night never strands a prize), and every racer still owed at the settle reads that it is waiting. The achievements are told by player id (`GameProgress.raceNightFinished(UUID, won)`) for every racer who started a race, wherever they are when the night settles, the boot's `StoredNight.raced` included. Each race is stored in one transaction (`EventDao`: its rows, the night's points, the season board, `races_done`); a stop, a reload that closes Race Night or Time Trials, or a crash (at the next boot, `StoredNight`) calls the night off with the races done standing, and a night called off before any race gives its prize slot back. Heats never go on course boards or records and never set a Cup time (their starts are grid spots), but each finished race counts once toward FINISH_COURSE. `/hcm reload` warns first while racers are at the track (`RaceNight.reloadWarning`). Its feed is the top-level `events` object (`EventFeed`), and its hub board is `@event`.

**The Weekly Cup (D2, WP-C; pure `games/cup`, live `games/cup/live`, `storage/CupDao`).** A course's Cup takes an entry (`games.cup.entry`, 10) once per player per course per week; the player's best counted time that week, from a run started after entering, is their Cup time. At the rollover (the quests' week start at 04:00, when Fresh Courses change) `CupRules.settle` shares the pool by Cup time: 70/30 with 2, 50/30/20 with 3 or more, rounded down with the rest to 1st, ties sharing their places; an entrant with no Cup time forfeits their entry to the pool; fewer than 2 Cup times refunds everyone; a deleted, changed (`CupLayout`) or closed course voids the week with refunds. The pool is every entry plus `server_topup` (20, twice the entry: nobody who sets a Cup time in a Cup of 2 or 3 gets back less than they paid) only when 2 or more set a Cup time, so the ledger over entries, prizes and refunds nets exactly the top-ups paid (`CupDaoTest`). Money moves only in `CupDao`: an entry is the debit and its row in one transaction; a settlement is every payout, the `cup_settlements` row (whose primary key refuses a second) and every entrant's queued line in one transaction, run at the rollover and at every boot, so a missed or crashed rollover is paid once. Cup prizes are outside the skill caps (it is the players' own pool). Which courses run one is `CupOptIn`: Fresh parkour, Sky Rings, Ice Boat and Dropper courses by default while Fresh Courses change once a week (`CupRules.freshEligible`), a hand-built course only after `/hcm games cup on`, an admin's choice kept per course id. The hooks are `CupLink`: the course screen's item at slot 24 and the party screen's at slot 44, the tile's NAME and lore from one Cup read (`CupLink.tile`), and `CupLink.finished` inside `settleCounted`, which solo and party-race runs reach and warm-ups and Race Night heats never do. `games.cup.enabled: false` stops new entries and hides it; running Cups still settle. A `games.cup` block that can't be read keeps settling on the shipped numbers with entries closed (`Game.opensOnDefaults`, the one game that stays open on its defaults). The owner's note: an entry pool can mean the youngest pay into a pool the oldest win; it is small, opt-in per player (`/hcm play cup off` hides it) and refunded when a player is alone.

**Falling Floors (§B.3, WP-F; `games/arena`, pure rules in `games/arena/rules`).** TNT Run without TNT: three glass floors in one 48 x 40 x 48 box (`origin`, kept 32 blocks from every Fresh area, the keep area and the Clubhouse's box to be built at all, 16 from hand-built courses; shipped 576 from everything, out of sight), a new shape each week (`ArenaPlanner`, algo 2: a glass walk), and a glass gallery that is the lobby, the stand and where the out go. `ArenaRound` is the pure round (RESET while the floors are put back and verified, LOBBY, COUNTDOWN, TELEPORT, HOLD for the 3-2-1, PLAYING, and CLOSED; a solo round when alone), `FloorRules` marks a block under a player and drops it `fade_ticks` later, and the edges fall in after `round_seconds`. `ArenaService` drives a round through `ArenaHost` (`LiveArenaHost` on the server, a fake in tests): a player the server couldn't move to a spot watches from the gallery, too few left calls the round off, and a countdown or solo round doesn't start when it might still be going at the next restart (`restartClose`). Between rounds `FloorWriter` puts every block of the box back within a per-tick budget, and nobody joins a round until the floors are verified; nobody can change a block in the box while the game is on. Rewards (`FloorsRewards`) are normal skill rewards under `daily_cap` (35, every milestone and the daily): the first full round of the day (5), and three solo milestones (5, 10, 15) paid whole or not at all (a milestone the day's caps can't pay waits for another day); a win pays nothing extra. Its feed entry is `kind: "arena"` with this week's `shape` and its solo board as `top`.

**No pushing (`games/NoPush`, `GamesService.noPush()`).** `Player.setCollidable(false)` stops mobs pushing a player but not another player: the client predicts player collisions and only a scoreboard team's COLLISION_RULE reaches it. So Falling Floors' round players, the Dropper's fallers, and parkour and elytra racers on Easy and Medium courses (`trial/Bumping`, the owner's decision after the 0.37.1 live test, for races: Hard and harder keep vanilla bumping, a course with no tier counts as Medium; never boats: boats bump, as the owner chose; a solo run is left as it always was) join one main-scoreboard team, `hcm_nopush` (COLLISION_RULE NEVER), and leave it on every way their round or run ends; the `setCollidable` calls stay, for mobs. A player is on one main-scoreboard team at a time, so `on` remembers the team they came from (another plugin's nametag colours, say) and `off` puts them back on it if it still exists; `on` is idempotent and `off` restores once. The remembered teams are memory only: `GamesService.stop` puts everyone back (`offAll`, after every game has stopped), and `start` empties the team (`clearAll`) with a WARN naming how many a crash left on it, whose earlier teams are lost. A player is in one world session at a time, so no two games hold the team for one player at once. Geyser carries a team's prefix, suffix, colour and name-tag rule to a Bedrock client but not its collision rule, and `setCollidable` is server-side only, so a Bedrock player's own client may still push itself off another player; the team stops the Java side.
<!-- ---- end events batch ---- -->

<!-- ---- the Clubhouse (CLUBHOUSE-SPEC, WP-CH) ---- -->
**The Clubhouse (`games/clubhouse`, with bridges in `trial`, `event` and `golf`).** One room in the Games world, a place inside the world sessions, not a new engine: `Clubhouse` is a `Game` (id `clubhouse`, kind TRIAL) whose players are in ordinary Clubhouse sessions, and a race hands a session over IN PLACE (`WorldSessions.passTo` / `SessionCore.passTo`: the live session's game, ref and hooks change; nothing is restored, saved or cleared), so waiting there, racing and coming back is one session and one set of saved things. The rest of the games reach it only through `Clubhouse.door(games)`, a guarded `ClubDoor` that is `null` while it is off, closed, or not built and checked (and each flow uses it only for a course in the Clubhouse's world, as a session teleport stays in its world): every hook in `RaceMode`, `PartyRaces`, `NightRunner`, `NightPorts` and `GolfGroups` falls back to its own behaviour on `null`, so "off means off" is one rule (pinned by `ClubhouseRacesTest` with a fake door, on and off). The room copies the Falling Floors arena: `ClubhousePlanner` is pure (golden hash, every op in the box), `ClubhouseRoom` claims an empty box (`gen.clubhouse.claim`), builds with Fresh Courses' `BuildJob`/`HalfWriter` and lets nobody in until a verify passes in this run; its box is a `Regions.Extra` (`ClubhouseRegions.extras`) checked with `extraProblem` against the Fresh slots and the keep area, `extraApartProblem` against the arena (each pair on its own, so a neighbour with a problem of its own still counts) and `extraHandBuiltProblem` against hand-built courses, and guarded like the arena; the other way round, Fresh Courses' vet, keep and check see it through `DailyCourses.extraBoxes` (`GenHost.extras`), and the arena's own check through `ArenaRegions.problems(..., others, ...)`, so the four kinds of place (slots, keep area, arena, Clubhouse) keep 32 apart whichever moves (`ExtraBoxesApartTest`; that is the build rule: the shipped spots are 576 apart, out of each other's sight). `ClubVisits` keeps who is in and why (visit, party, night, golf; watcher or not), the arrival sweep (an arrival that never lands leaves no ghost), `max_minutes` and the restart hold (home a minute after its warning and always `HOME_BEFORE_RESTART`, 5 s, before the restart's own minute; a race, Race Night or golf group already going that ends early in the hold still comes in, as CLUBHOUSE-SPEC §7 allows, and gets the same warning and minute; from `LAST_IN_BEFORE_RESTART`, 66 s, before the restart to the end of its minute, when an arrival could not get that minute, nobody is taken in: `ClubVisits.closedForRestart` through `ClubDoor.closingForRestart`, asked by `ClubRaces.toClub`, `ClubNight.takes` and `ClubGolf.take` before they touch anything, so racers and golfers go home with their own lines, and refused again in `Clubhouse.takeIn`; `CrossFeatureJourneyRestartHoldTest` walks both sides). The board is one tagged `TextDisplay` (`BoardDisplay`), redrawn only on a new result or, for a live race, at most once a second from small read APIs on `PartyRaces`, `NightRunner` and `GolfGroups`, read through `ClubRaces.live`, `ClubNight` and `ClubGolf` into `LiveRace`. **Watch live** (`WatchLive`) puts a Clubhouse visitor in SPECTATOR inside their own session: the session itself holds that game mode (`SessionCore.mode`, on the live session, so it dies with it; `BukkitPort` reads it, so the kit guard re-asserts spectator, not adventure, while they watch, and reports a mode the server didn't take), `WatchArea` is the pure box they may fly in (a slot or plot grown by 8, a hand-built course's marks grown by 16, a golf hole's bounds, a Race Night track; kept inside the world's heights), and the keeper CANCELS a move out of it (never a changed destination: that is an unarmed PLUGIN teleport, which the session takes as someone else's, a short hop voiding and a long one ending it), puts a watcher already outside back with the session's own armed teleport, cancels SPECTATE teleports outside (an inside one is re-made as the session's own next tick), and on a void puts them back in the area, never in the Clubhouse (`WatchKeeperSessionTest` walks it through `SessionCore`). Spectating an entity is allowed only for that race's racers. Every exit restores the saved state, game mode first (`SavedStateCodec.apply`), a crash boot included; a watcher is brought down to the session's start (the Clubhouse floor) before a restore, except while the server stops, and an exit with nothing to restore still takes the spectator mode away (`Port.resetMode`), as does a crash-join recovery that fails in place, once it has brought them down to the world's spawn. `WatchVisibility` hides watchers from everyone who isn't watching (hidePlayer/showPlayer, reconciled on joins, world changes and session starts) and shows them again on every way out. **Ride along** (`games/trial/Riders`, `RideAlong`) seats one passenger behind a boat driver from Time Trials' one `seat` path (every start, grid, re-grid and checkpoint re-seat), in a Time Trials session of the rider's own with no run, so nothing of theirs is timed, counted or paid; `WorldEntities.mayEnter` lets only the owner and their rider into a game boat, and the kit guard keeps a seated rider in. A rider is on the no-push team and not collidable for the whole ride (tracked apart from the pairing, so every end, the driver's included, takes both off), and off the boat is kept within 4 blocks of a parked driver. A ride that goes to the Clubhouse with its driver is kept (`Ride.inClub`) and handed back to Time Trials in place at the driver's next race from there. `games.trials.rider_runs_count: false` makes a run that had a rider at any point (`TrialRun.hadRider`, latched on boarding) just for fun: a solo run is VOID (`TimeTrials.withRider`), a party race keeps the place but skips the course's normal run; Race Night refuses riders. Ride along shows only while the Clubhouse is open (its door). **Kits never wipe an inventory**: a kit change strips kit items only and puts each without overwriting the player's own (`KitItems.put`), and a kit slot is emptied of a kit item only (`KitItems.clear`), in every game (the Dropper's kits, the elytra's rocket refill, warm-up slots and golf included; `KitSlotsTest` scans for any other inventory write); a hand-over to a race banks every non-kit item in the carry first (`SessionCore.bankExtras`), and hands nobody out when that fails, so an auction win or a Mini that arrives mid-session comes home once. Golf together's **Play again together** works from the Clubhouse: the host's Start hands each member waiting there to golf in place (`ClubGolf.fromClubhouse`). Since 0.38.3 courses and golf start through it (`solo_first`) and solo finishes come back to it (`solo_after`), with every fallback in `ClubRoute`. Its four boards (results, this week's best, now playing, and my stats, which only its viewer sees) hang FIXED on the walls; they read the database off the main thread, cached (`ClubReads`). Nothing in the Clubhouse moves tokens or counts toward a board, quest, achievement or the Cup. 0.38.4 adds the Play? screen every invite opens (`InviteAskMenu`), the kit's Challenge (a 1-vs-1 race party through `PartyRaces.challenge`), the NEW RECORD title and burst (`ClubDoor.soloResult`'s record line), the trophy case (`TrophyWall`), the jukebox corner (`JukeboxCorner`, `Songs`, planner ALGO 2) and the family week (`FamilyWeek`, `FamilyWeekRules`: points on `club:<weekKey>` boards of game `clubhouse`, 10 a day, never tokens).
<!-- ---- end the Clubhouse ---- -->

---
## 4. Configuration Schema (sketch)
```yaml
store:
  name: "Crate"
  display_url: "www.Crate.craft"   # fake TLD on purpose (never resolves)
market:
  elasticity: 0.05        # NOW scaled to each item's floor..ceiling range (Phase 2.5.1)
  inertia: 0.2
  integrated_bulk: true   # price moves across a multi-unit order (Phase 2.5.1)
  # per-item floor / ceiling / initial_stock / max_stock in the catalog
shipping:
  mode: PERCENTAGE        # PERCENTAGE | FLAT
  tiers:
    rush:      { real_hours: 24, percent: 20, flat: 500 }   # "Rush" = 1-day
    two_day:   { real_hours: 48, percent: 10, flat: 250, prime_flat: true }
    three_day: { real_hours: 72, percent: 0,  flat: 0 }     # free
  locker: { enabled: true }   # holds deliveries when offline / inventory full
marketplace:                  # the Pallet/Crate player-to-player market
  fee:
    commission_percent: 5     # % cut per sale (money sink) — DECISION default
    daily_storage_fee: 0      # optional per-Pallet/day; 0 = off
  require_protected_land: true
  ban_list: [ BEDROCK, COMMAND_BLOCK, BARRIER, STRUCTURE_BLOCK, "*_SPAWN_EGG", JIGSAW ]
  departments: [ BLOCKS, FOOD, TOOLS, WEAPONS, ARMOR, REDSTONE, COLLECTIBLES, MISC ]
  category_overrides:         # relocate the few that auto-sort wrong
    HONEYCOMB: MISC
courier:                      # the Deliveries Site (§3.10) — a money faucet, not a market
  payout: { base: 4.0, per_block: 0.012, min_distance: 200, max_distance: 4000 }
  travel:  { foot: 1.00, mount: 0.85, boat: 0.80, ghast: 0.70, rail: 0.65, elytra: 0.45, creative: 0.00 }
  anti_teleport: { floor: 0.70, payout: 0.20 }   # <70% of the distance tracked pays 20%
  bands:
    local:     { min: 200,  max: 600,  per_day: 2 }
    regional:  { min: 600,  max: 1800, per_day: 2 }
    long_haul: { min: 1800, max: 4000, per_day: 1 }
  expire_minutes: 60
  turn_in_radius: 10
worlds:
  economy_enabled_worlds: [ world, resource ]   # sandbox: NO market/marketplace in creative (§11)
crafting:
  respect_town_perms: true    # the Workbench is retired (no recipe); the PC recipe lives under recipes:
recipes:                      # every craftable block: vanilla SHAPED 3x3, reloadable, recipe-book unlocked
  pc:      { shape: ["GGG","CKC","IQI"], ingredients: { G: GLASS_PANE, C: COPPER_BLOCK, K: COMPARATOR, I: IRON_BLOCK, Q: QUARTZ_BLOCK } }
  printer: { shape: ["IHI","CSC","PAP"], ingredients: { … } }
  vending: { … }   # two-tall block
  pallet:  { shape: ["SSS","TBT","SSS"], ingredients: { S: "#wooden_slabs", T: STICK, B: BARREL } }   # "#tag" = any item of the tag
  arcade:  { … }
  mailbox: { wood: { … X: "#planks" … }, light_blue: { … X: LIGHT_BLUE_DYE … }, black: …, white: …, purple: …, blue: …, orange: …, yellow: … }
skins:                        # Base64 head values; blank = the block's plain base material
  pc: "…", printer: "…", arcade: "…"
  pallet_empty: "…", pallet_used: "…"          # a placed Pallet swaps as listings come/go
  vending_lower: "…", vending_upper: "…"       # the Vending Machine is two heads tall
  mailbox: { wood: "…", light_blue: "…", black: "…", white: "…", purple: "…", blue: "…", orange: "…", yellow: "…" }
minis:
  pricing_mode: ESCALATING    # FIXED | ESCALATING (overridable per series)
  rarity_styles:              # assign the TIER; color + defaults follow (edit palette once)
    LEGENDARY: { pane: YELLOW,     name_color: gold,         glint: true,  default_cap: 5,   default_price: 50000 }
    EPIC:      { pane: PURPLE,     name_color: light_purple, glint: true,  default_cap: 20,  default_price: 15000 }
    RARE:      { pane: BLUE,       name_color: aqua,         glint: false, default_cap: 100,  default_price: 4000 }
    UNCOMMON:  { pane: GREEN,      name_color: green,        glint: false, default_cap: 500,  default_price: 800 }
    COMMON:    { pane: LIGHT_GRAY, name_color: gray,         glint: false, default_cap: -1,   default_price: 150 }
  categories: [ ANIMAL, FOOD, LETTER, SYMBOL, CHARACTER, VEHICLE, HOLIDAY, MISC ]   # your "Type" list (open/editable)
  series:
    - name: "Woodland Critters"
      rarity: COMMON
      entries:
        # category = the "Type" (subject); type = the form (HEAD | ARMOR_STAND)
        - { name, type: HEAD, category: ANIMAL, texture, cap: -1, price, craftable: false, recipe: [],
            wild_drop: { source: BLOCK_BREAK, block: COBBLESTONE, chance: 0.000001, natural_only: true } }
```
---
## 5. Data Model / Persistence
SQLite via JDBC. Tables:
- **Market:** per-item current price + **stock** + **max_stock**; **price-history** snapshots.
- **Orders / Locker:** player, items, shipping tier, cost paid, placed-at + deliver-at timestamps, status (in-transit / in-locker / collected).
- **Marketplace:** Pallet locations + owners; listings (item, price, qty, seller); accrued fees.
- **Minis:** per-type minted count + circulation; per-individual unique ID, current owner, provenance/price history; Vending Machine listings; Auction House listings + escrowed bids + close times.
- **Custom blocks:** placed PC / Mini Workbench / Vending Machine / Display Case / **Pallet** locations + owners.
- **Courier sites (§3.10):** one row per placed delivery building — the region's origin and size, the template and rotation, the doorstep, the recipient villager's id, and `snapshot`, a GZIPped blob of the **original** blocks. Written before the first block changes and deleted only once the restore has run.
- **Courier (§3.10):** one row per job — player, type, state, band, accepted-at world/coords, waypoint coords, the **locked** distance, trade-run cargo + quoted value, the movement-statistic **snapshot** taken at acceptance, the UTC epoch-day it counts against, and its expiry. Daily band caps are counted from these rows; there is no separate tally table.
- **Daily limits:** per-player sell + buy counters (per-item too), reset daily (UTC).
- **Live market (schema v32, §3.1 "The live market (0.33)"):** per-item drift and cooldown clocks (`market_sim_state`), one row per event (`market_events`), the seed, schedule and counters (`market_sim_meta`), the daily sim-money ledger (`market_sim_ledger`), each player's last-seen news and mute (`market_news_seen`), and cached real-world closes (`market_real_quotes`). It holds no stock and no prices: those stay in the market tables and are only ever read.
- **Sound Mufflers (schema v33, §3.11):** `sound_mufflers`, one row per placed muffler keyed by world + block: owner, on/off, radius, Quieter percent, and the choices as text (`g <group> <LEVEL>` / `s <sound key> <LEVEL>` per line). The block itself is a `placed_blocks` row like every custom block. The two are reconciled on start.
- **The Games (schema v34, §3.12):** seven tables in one migration. `game_rounds`: one row per round of a game of chance with its seed, stake, payout and `data` (OPEN while a multi-step round is played, SETTLED once paid; the stake, the payout and the row land in one transaction), plus one DAILY row per player per cabinet per day for the scored daily try. `day` is the local epoch day and `opponent` the other player of a Coin Flip; partial unique indexes allow one OPEN round per player per game and one DAILY row per day. `game_breaks`: Take a break, one row per player: their own limit, a raise waiting and the day it starts, their pause, and the admin's limit and pause (-1 = no limit, -2 = nothing waiting). `game_scores`: each player's best per game and board, and how many runs. `game_rewards`: every skill reward paid; a one-time reward has a non-empty `ref` and the partial unique index `(player, game, kind, ref) WHERE ref <> ''` refuses it twice; `game` is `'*'` for the once-a-day-across-games kinds and `played` is the game that paid. `game_saved_state`: a player's things during a world game, one row per session, written before anything changes: the inventory and `carry` as blobs, XP, health, food, effects, game mode, flight and speeds, where they came from, and `phase` ACTIVE → RETURN → DONE; at most one live row per player, DONE rows pruned after a week. `game_courses`: world-game courses, time trials and golf in one table so an id is never used twice (`game` says whose; `data` is the game's own YAML; `rev` bumps in the same transaction as a layout edit). `game_prefs`: per-player choices (invites on or off, the golf ball, lines waiting for the next join). The secret behind the cabinets' daily seeds is one row in the existing `hcm_meta`.
Everything survives restarts. **Back up the DB before every migration (see §11).**
---
## 6. Phased Build Plan (for Claude Code)
Build and test each phase before the next.
- **Phase 1 — Skeleton + PC + Mini Workbench ✅ done, merged.**
- **Phase 2 — Basic Market Engine ✅ done, merged** *(abstract demand counter — superseded).*
- **Phase 2.5 — Finite-Stock Market Revision ✅ done, merged** (real positive stock, out-of-stock, per-item starting stock, spread, daily sell limit, price-history).
- **Phase 3 — Crate Store + Market GUIs ✅ done, merged** (`[www.Crate.craft](https://www.Crate.craft)` behind the PC, shipping tiers → Locker, restart-safe timers, clickable market GUI).
- **Phase 3.1 — Rebrand + config ✅ done, merged (`v0.3.1`):** on-screen "Amazon"→**Crate**; config-driven `store.name`/`store.display_url`; DESIGN.md v11 committed. *(Pricing proportional-elasticity + integrated-bulk fix rode in with Phase 3's Part A — confirm with the buy-600 test.)*
  - **Still-open polish:** the **GUI x600→"64" quantity fix** (show the real amount in the item name/lore); cosmetics — `api-version` (still `1.21`), the "(Phase 1)" log label, PC computer-head texture (send a Base64 value to wire it in).
- **Phase 4 — Minis core ✅ done, merged (`v0.4.0`):** config-driven catalog (`minis:` — `rarity_styles`, `categories`, `series→entries`), rarity→style (coloured name, glint, provenance tooltip), **minting as the single source of truth** (per-copy UUID anti-dupe + Mint #), caps enforced, circulation tracked, **Museum & Shop GUI** (mint via Vault), migration v7.
  - **Phase 4 follow-ups (a "Phase 4b"):** Mini Vending Machine, Auction House, **Wild Drops**, posed **armor-stand** spawning, and the minecraft-heads.com **web-import**.
- **Phase 5 — The Crate Marketplace:** Pallets, universal listings (everything sellable), **departments + auto-categorization + ban list**, fees, Minis in Collectibles. *(Can be pulled earlier if "sell anything" is wanted before Minis.)*
- **Phase 6 — Market Web Dashboard (§3.7):** the live stock-market website. *(Extension: a **transactional web shop** — secure `/crate web` login + buy-from-browser, delivering to the Locker — builds on this same embedded server.)*
- **Phase 7 — In-Game Displays (§3.8):** wall-mounted `TextDisplay` price panels, holographic tickers, sign boards.
- **Courier — Deliveries Site (§3.10) ✅ Phases 1 and 2 done (v16–v17):** job board, three distance bands with daily caps, rolled waypoints, the statistic-blended travel multiplier, the anti-teleport floor, trade runs that sell cargo into the market at the live rate — and a **vanilla village house with a villager** at the far end, placed on approach and restored from a snapshot when the run ends. *The crate landed in v17.3 and was priced in v17.4 — carried in hand, destroyed at the end of every run, with a loss fee, courier debt and a PC-bought replacement. Still open: the complete-a-delivery quest verb this unblocks, and the Fragile/Perishable cargo modifiers.*
- **Future — more PC Sites (§2.2):** Towny plots Site, etc.
- **Phase 8 — Rewards & Arcade (§3.9):** tokens (login streaks/playtime), loot boxes/crates, lotto/scratch tickets, the pity exchange, and the Arcade installation at the Mall. Reuses the drop/rarity/cap tech — mostly content.
- **Phase 9–11 — Arcade as a place + earning sources (§3.9):** the Arcade was built from placeable, owned/protected, skinnable **machine blocks** you right-click to play — a **Crate Machine**, **Scratch-Ticket Booth**, **Pity Exchange Kiosk**, and **Token Counter**. *(v13: these are retired in favour of the single Arcade hub block — see §3.9; placed ones keep working.)* Token earning now has four sources: login streaks, playtime, **one-time achievements** (first Mini, first sale, first PC, first crate, first pack, $10k), and **daily/weekly quests** (`/hcm quests` — repeatable objectives like "sell $500 to the market", "print a Mini", "open a crate/pack" that pay tokens on completion and reset each day/week). Every earn shows a "+N token" toast. Minting still happens only through the cap-aware Printer pipeline.
Then: retire DynamicShopGUI now that Phase 3's market GUI is live (server-side removal — not a code task).

## 7. Decisions
**Resolved:**
- **Plugin name:** HomeCraft Management (repo: github.com/Dierks27/HomeCraftMgmt).
- **Online store name:** **Crate**, shown as **`[www.Crate.craft](https://www.Crate.craft)`** (fake TLD, safe). Fast tier = **Rush**; seller box = **Pallet**; delivery inbox = **Locker**.
- **Everything is sellable** via the Crate Marketplace (Pallets); the **house market stays lean** (curated staples only).
- **Auto-categorization** into departments via the game's item categories + tags, with admin override + **ban list**.
- **PC = a browser; features are Sites** (Crate now; Towny plots + dashboard later).
- **Minis** are sellable (Collectibles department) via Vending Machine (fixed) + Auction House (bidding).
- **Marketplace fee = small % commission** (default), optional per-Pallet daily storage fee.
- **Pricing:** finite-stock, **elasticity scaled to each item's range**, **integrated bulk pricing**, buy/sell spread, daily sell + buy limits with per-item caps.
- Minis: configurable mint caps, fixed/escalating pricing, per-entry craftable flag, custom Mini Workbench, admin-defined empty-by-default recipes.
- Shipping: real 1/2/3-day at 20% / 10% / free (percentage default; flat/Prime available).
**Still open:**
- **Marketplace pricing** — seller-set fixed price (rec) vs. optional dynamic drift.
- **PC protection** — respect Towny/WG perms (rec: yes).
- **Series concepts & rarity names** — creative call (ongoing).
---
## 8. Notes for the Implementer
- Collectibles cover **both heads and armor stands**; catalog is **admin-curated via checkmark import** (no blind sweep).
- Reference DynamicShopGUI's pricing for concepts; write original code — we're eliminating that dependency.
- Keep modules loosely coupled so phases ship independently; the PC's **Site launcher** keeps future features pluggable.
- All player-facing money flows through Vault.
- **Config everything (standing principle):** anything an admin might ever change lives in `config.yml` and **reloads live** (`/hcm reload`) — no hardcoded values. Hold this for every future phase.
- Do **not** touch QuickShop.
---
## 9. Integration Reference — how we hook every external system
> Verify exact signatures against the installed versions (the "formalities" pass).
### 9.1 Vault (economy) — required for all money
Grab the `Economy` service from Bukkit's ServicesManager on enable; use `has()`, `withdrawPlayer()`, `depositPlayer()` for orders, shipping, marketplace fees, Mini minting, vending, auction escrow, Mall rent. Soft-depend; disable money features + warn if absent.
### 9.2 Towny — the Mall + build permissions + (future) Towny Site
Use `TownyAPI`: resolve `TownBlock`/`Town`/`Resident` and check build permission before placing/using custom blocks (PC, Workbench, **Pallet**, Vending Machine); the Mall uses Towny's `/plot forrent`; the future Towny Site reads plots-for-sale data. Soft-depend.
### 9.3 WorldGuard / WorldEdit — region build permissions
Query the `RegionContainer` and test BUILD for the player before placement/interaction. Soft-depend.
### 9.4 LuckPerms — permissions
Declare nodes in `plugin.yml`; check `player.hasPermission(...)`. Node list in §10.
### 9.5 PlaceholderAPI — data display + in-game tickers
Register a `PlaceholderExpansion` (identifier `hcm`) exposing e.g. `%hcm_price_<item>%`, `%hcm_stock_<item>%`, `%hcm_trend_<item>%`, `%hcm_mini_minted_<id>%`, `%hcm_mini_circulation_<id>%`, `%hcm_order_status%`. Powers TAB/holograms/sign boards (§3.8). Soft-depend.
### 9.7 ProtocolLib — the Sound Muffler (§3.11)
`net.dmulloy2:ProtocolLib:5.4.0` (Maven Central), compileOnly and soft-depend. One `PacketAdapter` (`muffler/MufflerPacketListener`) on `NAMED_SOUND_EFFECT`, `ENTITY_SOUND` and `WORLD_EVENT`, loaded only when ProtocolLib is enabled. The sound key comes from `getSoundEffects()` + `Registry.SOUNDS.getKey`, falling back to the holder's text for a resource-pack sound. Without ProtocolLib, mufflers place and save but hush nothing, and the menu says so.
### 9.6 Native Paper/Bukkit APIs we rely on
- **Custom heads (Minis):** `PlayerProfile` + `PlayerTextures`.
- **Armor-stand Minis:** spawn + configure `ArmorStand` from stored data.
- **Item/block tagging:** `PersistentDataContainer` (Mini IDs, PC/Workbench/**Pallet** markers).
- **Auto-categorization:** item **creative-category / item-group** API + Minecraft item **tags** + property flags (verify exact 26.2 API).
- **GUIs:** `InventoryHolder` menus (PC Site launcher, Crate store, Museum & Shop, Vending Machine, Auction House, Pallet).
- **Displays:** wall-mounted `TextDisplay` price panels + holograms (text-display entities); PAPI powers tickers/TAB.
- **Recipes:** inside the Workbench GUI (config-driven), NOT vanilla recipes.
- **Timers:** Bukkit scheduler + durable SQLite timestamps.
- **Storage:** SQLite via JDBC.

## 10. Commands & Permission Nodes
**Commands (most interaction is block/GUI-based, admin commands aside):**
- `/hcm reload`, `/hcm admin …` (curate Minis, caps/prices/series, give items, Mall anchor, manage departments/ban list), `/hcm market …` (admin/test buy/sell/price/list/history).
- `/hcm give <printer|pc|vending|display|mailbox [variant]|pallet|arcade|muffler> [player]` and `/hcm give <card <id>|pack <id>|binder|filament <color> <n>> [player]` (admin). The Mailbox variant is one of `wood` (default), `light_blue`, `black`, `white`, `purple`, `blue`, `orange`, `yellow`. **Not given out any more:** the Auction House block (use `/hcm auction`) and the four Arcade machine blocks (the Arcade hub covers them).
- `/hcm auction` — the Mini Auction House (the only way to reach it).
- `/hcm museum [id]` — the browse-only Mini Museum; with an id, straight onto that Mini's detail card (the click target of found-broadcasts).
- `/hcm courier` — the Courier job board (§3.10). Also a Site on the PC.
- `/hcm market news [on|off]` (0.33, everyone, `hcm.market.price`) — "Right now" (HOT/DEAL with time left, and the season), then the last 8 headlines from 7 days with their ages; `on`/`off` turns market news in your own chat on or off. `/hcm market price <item>` also shows the usual price, the mood and any badge while the live market runs.
- `/hcm market news <item> <up|down|wanted> [percent] [quiet]` and `/hcm market sim status [item] | hot|deal <item> [percent] [hours] | stop <item|all> | reset <item|all> | pause | resume | preview <item> [days] | audit [days] | real status|test <symbol>|fetch` (0.33, admin, `hcm.market.sim`) — run and test the live market (§3.1). A forced flash is held to 10–25%, a forced HOT/DEAL starts at full strength (≤ 15%), and both keep the headroom check and are refused when the move that would really happen is under 5%. `reset all` asks for a confirm within 10 s. `preview` draws one possible future from a throwaway random source and changes nothing. Every forced event is logged with who forced it and carries `source: admin`.
- `/hcm play [game|course]`, `/hcm play break`, `/hcm play accept|deny`, `/hcm play invites [on|off]` and `/hcm leave` (0.35, everyone, `hcm.games.play`) — the Games screen, or open a game (rules and odds first) or a course (a time trial skips its course screen from a command or a join sign, first asking "Warm up (3:00)" or "Go straight to the timed run" while warm-ups are on (never on a Dropper), and opens its course screen from a tile; a golf course always opens its course screen); Take a break; answer an invite; invites on or off for the friend games, party races, rides and golf together (`off` also turns Coin Flip invites off, which only the Take a break screen turns on); leave a world game, which also finishes a trip home that didn't complete. `/hcm play blackjack` opens Twenty-One. `accept`, `deny`, `break`, `leave`, `invites`, `news`, `cup`, `watch`, `cheer`, `cheers`, `rider` and the Fresh Courses ids are reserved, so no course may use them. In a world game every other `/hcm` subcommand is refused ("Finish or leave your game first — /hcm leave").
- `/hcm play <game|course> <player>` (0.35, `hcm.games.admin` or the console) — the same for someone else: NPCs, command blocks, a hub.
- `/hcm games status | feature <game|course|auto> | break <player> show|pause <days>|limit <tokens|none>|clear|clear-own confirm | scores reset <game> [board|all] [player] [confirm] | saved <player> show|restore|return|discard confirm | course … | golf …` (0.35, `hcm.games.admin`) — run the Games (§3.12); the `course` and `golf` editors are the next two items. `status` gives every game open or closed and why, live sessions, saved states waiting, open rounds, today's pick, and the next scheduled restart and when its hold starts; `feature` writes `games.featured` and never takes a game of chance, and `feature trials` or `feature golf` makes every course of that game the pick; `break` works on offline players (a parent's controls) and `clear-own` needs `confirm` and logs a WARNING; `scores reset` and `saved … discard` count or explain first. Every use is logged with who did it, the mini golf editor's saved edits included.
- `/hcm games course list | create <id> <parkour|elytra|boat> [tier] | <id> [info] | <id> start [confirm] | <id> checkpoint add [radius] [confirm] | <id> checkpoint remove <n> [confirm] | <id> checkpoint list | <id> finish [radius] [confirm] | <id> fall <y|off> [confirm] | <id> tier <easy|medium|hard|extreme> | <id> name <words…> | <id> minseconds <n|default> | <id> enable|disable | <id> tp | <id> test | <id> feature [off] | <id> delete confirm` (0.35, `hcm.games.admin`) — the time-trial course editor (§3.12), done standing in a `games.worlds` world. Radii default to 1.5 (parkour), 4 (elytra) and 3 (boat), kept to 0.5-16, at most 128 checkpoints (`Course.MAX_CHECKPOINTS`, 64 before 0.37); `minseconds` is 0-3600. The layout edits (start, checkpoint add/remove, finish, fall) bump `rev` and clear the all-time and this week's times, and take the trailing `confirm` only when there are times to lose. A fall height must sit under the course's lowest point and not under the world's floor, and a start, checkpoint or finish at or under it is refused. A course can't be called `auto`. A command that throws is logged SEVERE and answered "That didn't work - see the console.", never switching time trials off. `enable` needs a start, a finish and a world in `games.worlds`; `test` runs any complete course and records nothing; `feature` pins the course of the week (`off` unpins); `delete confirm` removes the course and every board of it.
- `/hcm games golf list | create <id> [name…] | <id> [info|list] | <id> tp [hole] | <id> hole add <par> [confirm] | <id> hole <n> cup|tee|remove [confirm] | <id> hole <n> par <2-6> | <id> hole <n> bounds <1|2> [confirm] | <id> name <name…> | <id> enable|disable | <id> delete confirm` (0.35, `hcm.games.admin`) — the mini golf course editor (§3.12), done standing on the course. `create` makes a closed course in the `games.worlds` world the admin stands in; `hole add` puts the tee at their feet facing the way they face (at most 18 holes); `cup` is the block they look at within 6, the block the ball rests on (the floor of a sunken hole, a slab, a block flush with the green), and it refuses air, anything under 0.2 high (a carpet) and hollow blocks (a cauldron); `bounds 1|2` is a corner at their feet. Tee, cup, bounds and adding or removing a hole are layout: they bump `rev` and clear the course's high scores, after a `confirm` when there are scores to lose, and end every round on the course at once. `enable` needs every hole complete; `disable` and `delete confirm` send anyone playing the course, or on the way in, home. The ids `create`, `list`, `help` and `auto` are refused. Every saved edit is logged at INFO with who made it, and a command that throws answers in red instead of switching golf off.
- `/hcm play race [course]`, `/hcm play cup [on|off]`, `/hcm play golf <course>` and `/hcm play falling_floors` (the events batch, everyone, `hcm.games.play`) — the Race Night screen, or a party race on a time-trial course (never a Dropper); your Weekly Cups this week, or hide/show the Cup on your course screens; a golf course's screen with Play with friends; straight into the Falling Floors gallery (`tnt_run` is an alias) (§3.12, the events batch).
- `/hcm games cup status [course] | on|off|default <course> [confirm] | settle <course> [confirm] | void <course> [confirm]` (the events batch, `hcm.games.admin`) — the Weekly Cup: its state and every course running one; a course's switch (`off` with entrants asks for `confirm`, then calls this week's off and refunds first); pay a running Cup out now; call it off with refunds.
- `/hcm games event status | list [days] | start [course] [races N] [laps N] [in M] [fun] | go | cancel [confirm] | skip <id|next> | unskip <id> | pause | resume | results [id] | grid <course> show|auto|add|remove <n>|clear | stand <course> set|clear` (the events batch, `hcm.games.admin`) — run Race Night and set up a hand-built track's grid and viewing stand.
- `/hcm games floors [status] | reset | claim [confirm] | tp` (the events batch, `hcm.games.admin`) — Falling Floors: its state, put the floors back now, claim its box, watch from the gallery (refused until the floors are built and checked).
- `/hcm candy` (0.37.1, everyone, `hcm.arcade.use`) — your candy apples, your Trick-or-Treat Bag and the next shower; a bag that is owed is tried again, after the window too. `/hcm candy now [player] | status | clear | bag <player> | add <player> <n>` (0.37.1, `hcm.admin`; no new nodes) — run the Candy Apple shower (§3.9): `now` showers everyone online (or the named player) at once, one line each, "showered" or the skip reason, and is refused outside the window or while off; `status` gives on or off, the window and its zone, the next shower (and one a restart skips), the candy out, each online player's count, candy out, idle minutes and bag, and when this season's collectors leave the shop, with the zone; `clear` removes every candy out, counting nothing; `bag` gives a bag back, free (room needed, home from every game), and sets its mark; `add` adds 1-200 to a count and runs the goal check, to test the bag without collecting 31. Both are logged.
- `/hcm arcade odds` (0.35: opened to `hcm.arcade.use`) — players get one line per open game of chance (what it gives back, the lowest over its stakes, and how many plays a day); `hcm.admin` also gets the per-stake detail and the Scratch Ticket and crate report as before. Without `hcm.games.chance` it says games of chance aren't open to you.
- `/hcm guide [tokens|minis|wild|arcade|games]` — How It Works; `games` is the fifth page (0.35).
- `/hcm looks [renew <id>|gift <id> <player>]` (0.38, `hcm.arcade.use`) — My Looks, the look screen, Give a present on a look; `/hcm trail` with nothing after it opens My Looks (it was a toggle), `/hcm trail <id>|off|toggle` are unchanged, `/hcm trail next` goes to the next trail, `/hcm trail hide|show` hides or shows other people's trails, marks and parties. `/hcm looks list|give|end <player> ...`, `/hcm looks preview <id>` and `/hcm account move <from> <to> [confirm]` (0.38, `hcm.admin`) — the wardrobe's admin tools and the account move (every economy record of one UUID to another, one transaction).
- `/trade [<player>|accept|deny|cancel|on|off]` and `/hcm trade ...` (0.38, `hcm.trade.use`) — the trade window (§3.4, §3.9); `/hcm trades [player] [n] | show <id> | settle <id> release|void` (0.38, `hcm.admin`) — the trade log, and a trade the server didn't see finish.
- `/minis` (or Mall block) — Museum & Shop.
- PC / Workbench / Vending Machine / Auction House / **Pallet** interaction = **right-click the block**.
- Avoid colliding with existing commands (e.g. QuickShop's `/qs finditem`).
**Permission nodes (LuckPerms-manageable):**
- `hcm.admin`
- `hcm.pc.use`, `hcm.pc.craft`
- `hcm.workbench.place`, `hcm.workbench.use`
- `hcm.market.order` (all — **sell** only); `hcm.market.buy` (**op** — buying by command skips shipping, so it must never be open to players); `hcm.market.list` (op — full catalog dump) / `hcm.market.price` (all — one item + `/hcm balance`), both children of the back-compat parent `hcm.use`
- `hcm.marketplace.sell` (place/use a Pallet), `hcm.marketplace.buy`
- `hcm.mini.sell`, `hcm.mini.craft` (`hcm.mini.buy` retired with the browse-only Museum)
- `hcm.vending.create`, `hcm.auction.list`, `hcm.auction.bid`
- **Player-facing openers (all `default: true`, v16):** `hcm.auction.list` (`/hcm auction`), `hcm.museum.use` (`/hcm museum [id]`), `hcm.arcade.use` (`/hcm arcade`), `hcm.quests.use` (`/hcm quests`), `hcm.courier.use` (`/hcm courier`). These subcommands open a player-facing GUI — read-only, token-only, or (the Courier) earn-only — and are the documented player route (§3.2); the nodes exist so an admin can withdraw one without removing the feature, not because they are restricted by default.
- `hcm.mall.rent` (or delegate to Towny)
- `hcm.market.limit.bypass` (op, a child of `hcm.admin`) — skips the daily buy/sell limits and per-item caps, **but not the live market's event limits** (the HOT/UP sell limit and the DEAL/DOWN buy limit bind everyone, §3.1)
- `hcm.market.sim` (0.33; op, a child of `hcm.admin`) — run and test the live market: forced flashes and HOT/DEALs, stop, reset, pause/resume, preview, audit, real-world tests
- `hcm.muffler.use` (all, a child of `hcm.admin`) — place a Sound Muffler and change your own; anyone may look at one, `hcm.admin` may change any (§3.11)
- `hcm.games.play` (0.35; all, a child of `hcm.admin`) — play the Games: `/hcm play`, the Games screen, the cabinets, courses and mini golf, invites, Take a break and `/hcm leave`. The games open only once `games.enabled: true`, but Take a break and `/hcm leave` work while they are off. Race Night's chat lines and join bar reach only players who have it
- `hcm.games.chance` (0.35; all, a child of `hcm.admin`) — the games of chance. Revoke it for a group to keep them out of every game of chance, the Scratch Ticket, Crates and token Card Packs included. While the games are on, the Games screen and the Arcade don't show them the games of chance, the crates or the Scratch Ticket, and Tab after `/hcm play` doesn't offer the games of chance. Token Card Packs still show in the pack shop, and so do the crates and the Scratch Ticket while the games are off. Trying one says "Games of chance aren't open to you." Skill games are unaffected
- `hcm.trade.use` (0.38; all) — the trade window: `/trade`, `/hcm trade`, the crouch gesture and the PC's Trade site. A player without it can't trade and can't be asked
- `hcm.games.admin` (0.35; op, a child of `hcm.admin`) — `/hcm games …`, `/hcm play <game> <player>`, writing `[Arcade]` join signs, and building in the Games worlds while the games are on
